{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SFaqd7gWhpfAiLybgp2MCa","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"X-VLA Vision Token Pruning Evaluation on LIBERO.\n2\t\n3\tPrunes vision tokens by L2 norm after Florence2's image encoder + linear projection,\n4\tbefore they enter the Florence2 language encoder. Evaluates on LIBERO-Object.\n5\t\n6\tUsage:\n7\t    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 MUJOCO_GL=egl python eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py \\\n8\t        --model_path 2toINF/X-VLA-Libero \\\n9\t        --suite libero_object \\\n10\t        --keep_ratio 0.5 \\\n11\t        --num_episodes 50 \\\n12\t        --gpu 0\n13\t\"\"\"\n14\t\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\timport argparse\n18\timport json\n19\timport logging\n20\timport os\n21\timport sys\n22\timport time as time_mod\n23\tfrom pathlib import Path\n24\tfrom functools import wraps\n25\t\n26\tos.environ.setdefault(\"MUJOCO_GL\", \"egl\")\n27\t\n28\timport numpy as np\n29\timport torch\n30\tfrom PIL import Image\n31\t\n32\tsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\n33\t\n34\tlogging.basicConfig(\n35\t    level=logging.INFO,\n36\t    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n37\t    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n38\t)\n39\tlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n40\t\n41\tSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\n42\t    \"libero_spatial\": 280,\n43\t    \"libero_object\": 280,\n44\t    \"libero_goal\": 300,\n45\t    \"libero_10\": 520,\n46\t    \"libero_90\": 400,\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef parse_args():\n51\t    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"X-VLA pruning eval on LIBERO\")\n52\t    p.add_argument(\"--model_path\", type=str, default=\"2toINF/X-VLA-Libero\",\n53\t                   help=\"HuggingFace model path or local dir\")\n54\t    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_object\",\n55\t                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\n56\t    p.add_argument(\"--keep_ratio\", type=float, default=1.0,\n57\t                   help=\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\")\n58\t    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=50)\n59\t    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n60\t    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n61\t    p.add_argument(\"--action_horizon\", type=int, default=10,\n62\t                   help=\"How many actions from each chunk to execute before re-planning\")\n63\t    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None)\n64\t    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None)\n65\t    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=None)\n66\t    p.add_argument(\"--domain_id\", type=int, default=0)\n67\t    p.add_argument(\"--denoise_steps\", type=int, default=10,\n68\t                   help=\"Flow matching denoising steps\")\n69\t    return p.parse_args()\n70\t\n71\t\n72\t# --- Pruning hook ---\n73\t\n74\tdef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float):\n75\t    \"\"\"Monkey-patch _encode_image to prune vision tokens by L2 norm.\"\"\"\n76\t    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n77\t        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n78\t        return\n79\t\n80\t    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\n81\t\n82\t    @wraps(original_encode)\n83\t    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\n84\t        features = original_encode(pixel_values)  # [B, N, D]\n85\t        N = features.shape[1]\n86\t        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n87\t        if k >= N:\n88\t            return features\n89\t        norms = features.norm(dim=-1)  # [B, N]\n90\t        _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n91\t        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n92\t        pruned = features.gather(\n93\t            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\n94\t        )\n95\t        return pruned\n96\t\n97\t    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\n98\t    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f\", keep_ratio)\n99\t\n100\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01XbF6stedgmyjhmPn1Xcp4k","session_id":"d75c42b7-1d15-4ab5-b75d-33f4c8f9f7de","uuid":"1e1145be-6347-4c85-bf47-27d1cca366cf","timestamp":"2026-05-20T05:58:44.889Z"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01521F2nXRJjRC29Vg3czEug","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Token Importance Analysis: attention weight distribution + gradient correlation.\n2\t\n3\tAnalyzes tokens deleted vs retained by ACIS:\n4\t  1. Attention weight distribution (how much each token group is attended to in VLM layers)\n5\t  2. Gradient correlation (ACIS scores vs action-loss gradient magnitude)\n6\t  3. Per-sample raw data for visualization\n7\t\n8\tUsage:\n9\t    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python eval_token_importance.py \\\n10\t        --checkpoint checkpoints/imm_anchor_v8b_twostage/final/checkpoint.pt \\\n11\t        --num_samples 500 \\\n12\t        --output_dir results/token_importance_analysis/\n13\t\"\"\"\n14\t\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\timport argparse\n18\timport json\n19\timport logging\n20\timport sys\n21\tfrom pathlib import Path\n22\tfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple\n23\t\n24\timport numpy as np\n25\timport torch\n26\timport torch.nn.functional as F\n27\timport yaml\n28\t\n29\tARTIFACTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n30\tsys.path.insert(0, str(ARTIFACTS_DIR))\n31\t\n32\tlogging.basicConfig(\n33\t    level=logging.INFO,\n34\t    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n35\t    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n36\t)\n37\tlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n38\t\n39\t\n40\tdef parse_args():\n41\t    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Token importance analysis\")\n42\t    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str, required=True)\n43\t    parser.add_argument(\"--config\", type=str, default=None,\n44\t                        help=\"Config YAML (auto-detected from checkpoint if not provided)\")\n45\t    parser.add_argument(\"--num_samples\", type=int, default=500)\n46\t    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, default=\"results/token_importance_analysis/\")\n47\t    parser.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=4)\n48\t    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n49\t    return parser.parse_args()\n50\t\n51\t\n52\tdef find_config(checkpoint_path: str) -> str:\n53\t    ckpt_path = Path(checkpoint_path)\n54\t    for parent in ckpt_path.parents:\n55\t        for cfg in parent.glob(\"*.yaml\"):\n56\t            return str(cfg)\n57\t    return str(ARTIFACTS_DIR / \"configs\" / \"imm_anchor.yaml\")\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef collect_vlm_attention_weights(model, batch, vis_token_count):\n61\t    \"\"\"Register hooks on VLM attention layers to capture attention weights\n62\t    directed at visual token positions.\n63\t\n64\t    Returns: list of (B, N_vis) tensors — mean attention received by each\n65\t    visual token position, one per VLM layer.\n66\t    \"\"\"\n67\t    bm = model.base_model\n68\t    vlm = bm.vlm_with_expert\n69\t\n70\t    text_model = None\n71\t    if hasattr(vlm, 'text_model'):\n72\t        text_model = vlm.text_model\n73\t    elif hasattr(vlm, 'model'):\n74\t        text_model = vlm.model\n75\t\n76\t    if text_model is None:\n77\t        return []\n78\t\n79\t    layers = None\n80\t    if hasattr(text_model, 'layers'):\n81\t        layers = text_model.layers\n82\t    elif hasattr(text_model, 'model') and hasattr(text_model.model, 'layers'):\n83\t        layers = text_model.model.layers\n84\t\n85\t    if layers is None:\n86\t        return []\n87\t\n88\t    attention_maps = []\n89\t    hooks = []\n90\t\n91\t    def make_hook(layer_idx):\n92\t        def hook_fn(module, args, kwargs, output):\n93\t            if isinstance(output, tuple) and len(output) >= 2 and output[1] is not None:\n94\t                attn_w = output[1]  # (B, H, seq, seq)\n95\t                attention_maps.append((layer_idx, attn_w.detach()))\n96\t        return hook_fn\n97\t\n98\t    for idx, layer in enumerate(layers):\n99\t        attn_module = None\n100\t        if hasattr(layer, 'self_attn'):\n101\t            attn_module = layer.self_attn\n102\t        elif hasattr(layer, 'attention'):\n103\t            attn_module = layer.attention\n104\t\n105\t        if attn_module is not None:\n106\t            h = attn_module.register_forward_hook(make_hook(idx), with_kwargs=True)\n107\t            hooks.append(h)\n108\t\n109\t    return hooks, attention_maps\n110\t\n111\t\n112\tdef compute_gradient_importance(model, batch, device):\n113\t    \"\"\"Compute gradient magnitude of action loss w.r.t. each visual token.\n114\t\n115\t    Returns:\n116\t        grad_magnitudes: (B, N_vis) gradient L2 norm per token\n117\t        importance_scores: (B, N_vis) ACIS scores\n118\t        action_loss: scalar\n119\t    \"\"\"\n120\t    bm = model.base_model\n121\t\n122\t    images, img_masks = model.policy.prepare_images(batch)\n123\t    state = model.policy.prepare_state(batch)\n124\t\n125\t    from lerobot.utils.constants import OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS, OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK\n126\t    lang_tokens = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS]\n127\t    lang_masks = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK]\n128\t    actions = model.policy.prepare_action(batch)\n129\t\n130\t    vis_tokens, tokens_per_cam = model._get_visual_tokens(images)\n131\t    vis_tokens = vis_tokens.detach().requires_grad_(True)\n132\t\n133\t    noise = bm.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\n134\t    time = bm.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\n135\t    time_expanded = time[:, None, None]\n136\t    x_t = time_expanded * noise + (1 - time_expanded) * actions\n137\t    u_t = noise - actions\n138\t\n139\t    suffix_embs, suffix_pad, suffix_att = bm.embed_suffix(x_t, time)\n140\t\n141\t    importance_scores = model.acis(suffix_embs, vis_tokens)\n142\t\n143\t    N_vis = vis_tokens.shape[1]\n144\t    k = max(1, int(N_vis * model.k_ratio))\n145\t\n146\t    selected_tokens, mask, sel_info = model.token_selector(\n147\t        importance_scores, vis_tokens, k, training=True\n148\t    )\n149\t\n150\t    prefix_embs, prefix_pad, prefix_att = model._build_prefix_with_vis_tokens("}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01XbF6stedgmyjhmPn1Xcp4k","session_id":"d75c42b7-1d15-4ab5-b75d-33f4c8f9f7de","uuid":"6c2d6065-3cee-422f-8f7d-7b6779a70557","timestamp":"2026-05-20T05:58:45.260Z"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013dFwUmYq7sj4avvLYPhZkC","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/bin/bash\n2\t# X-VLA Pruning Sweep on LIBERO-Object\n3\t# Runs keep_ratio values sequentially on GPU 0\n4\t\n5\tset -e\n6\t\n7\texport MUJOCO_GL=egl\n8\texport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0\n9\texport TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1\n10\texport HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1\n11\texport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\n12\t\n13\tsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh\n14\tconda activate base\n15\t\n16\tcd /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress\n17\t\n18\tMODEL_PATH=\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/models--2toINF--X-VLA-Libero/snapshots/129e71460678b7236cee6fc9707f09d9fa0c3590\"\n19\tSUITE=\"libero_object\"\n20\tEPISODES=50\n21\tSEED=42\n22\tDOMAIN_ID=0\n23\t\n24\tK_VALUES=\"1.0 0.95 0.9 0.7 0.5 0.3 0.2 0.1\"\n25\t\n26\techo \"===== X-VLA Pruning Sweep =====\"\n27\techo \"Suite: $SUITE | Episodes/task: $EPISODES | Seed: $SEED\"\n28\techo \"K values: $K_VALUES\"\n29\techo \"\"\n30\t\n31\tfor K in $K_VALUES; do\n32\t    echo \">>> Starting keep_ratio=$K at $(date)\"\n33\t    python eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py \\\n34\t        --model_path \"$MODEL_PATH\" \\\n35\t        --suite \"$SUITE\" \\\n36\t        --keep_ratio \"$K\" \\\n37\t        --num_episodes \"$EPISODES\" \\\n38\t        --seed \"$SEED\" \\\n39\t        --domain_id \"$DOMAIN_ID\" \\\n40\t        --gpu 0 \\\n41\t        --output \"eval_results/xvla_prune_k${K}_${SUITE}.json\" \\\n42\t        2>&1 | tee \"eval_results/xvla_prune_k${K}_${SUITE}.log\"\n43\t    echo \">>> Finished keep_ratio=$K at $(date)\"\n44\t    echo \"\"\n45\tdone\n46\t\n47\techo \"===== All sweeps complete =====\"\n48\t\n49\t# Collect results summary\n50\tpython3 -c \"\n51\timport json, glob\n52\tresults = {}\n53\tfor f in sorted(glob.glob('eval_results/xvla_prune_k*_${SUITE}.json')):\n54\t    with open(f) as fh:\n55\t        d = json.load(fh)\n56\t    k = d['keep_ratio']\n57\t    results[k] = {\n58\t        'sr': d['overall_success_rate'],\n59\t        'ci': d['wilson_ci_95'],\n60\t        'tokens_kept': d['n_vision_tokens_kept'],\n61\t        'tokens_total': d['n_vision_tokens_original'],\n62\t    }\n63\t\n64\tprint('\\n===== X-VLA Pruning Results Summary =====')\n65\tprint(f'{'k':>6} {'SR%':>8} {'95% CI':>16} {'Tokens':>12}')\n66\tprint('-' * 46)\n67\tfor k in sorted(results.keys(), reverse=True):\n68\t    r = results[k]\n69\t    print(f'{k:>6.2f} {r[\\\"sr\\\"]*100:>7.1f}% [{r[\\\"ci\\\"][0]*100:.1f}%, {r[\\\"ci\\\"][1]*100:.1f}%] {r[\\\"tokens_kept\\\"]:>4}/{r[\\\"tokens_total\\\"]:>4}')\n70\t\"\n71\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01XbF6stedgmyjhmPn1Xcp4k","session_id":"d75c42b7-1d15-4ab5-b75d-33f4c8f9f7de","uuid":"deefe833-dbf0-417b-a330-818e89750923","timestamp":"2026-05-20T05:58:51.108Z"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WqUo6i4fQE31tMk7AnYtqd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-step Action Divergence Analysis: Baseline vs ACIS-pruned model.\n2\t\n3\tDrives the environment with baseline actions while recording both models'\n4\taction outputs at each timestep (same observation). Produces divergence curves\n5\tand per-dimension breakdown.\n6\t\n7\tUsage:\n8\t    python analyze_action_divergence.py --gpu 2 --num_episodes 5 --task_ids 0,1,2\n9\t\"\"\"\n10\t\n11\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n12\t\n13\timport argparse\n14\timport json\n15\timport logging\n16\timport os\n17\timport sys\n18\timport time as time_mod\n19\tfrom pathlib import Path\n20\t\n21\tos.environ.setdefault(\"MUJOCO_GL\", \"egl\")\n22\tos.environ.setdefault(\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\n23\tos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\n24\t\n25\timport matplotlib\n26\tmatplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n27\timport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n28\timport numpy as np\n29\timport torch\n30\t\n31\tARTIFACTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n32\tsys.path.insert(0, str(ARTIFACTS_DIR))\n33\t\n34\tlogging.basicConfig(\n35\t    level=logging.INFO,\n36\t    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n37\t    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n38\t)\n39\tlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n40\t\n41\t# Paths\n42\tBASELINE_CKPT = \"checkpoints/libero_full_ft_100k/checkpoint.pt\"\n43\tACIS_CKPT = \"checkpoints/libero_acis_stage1only_k07/stage1_done/checkpoint.pt\"\n44\tBASELINE_CONFIG = \"configs/libero_baseline.yaml\"\n45\tACIS_CONFIG = \"configs/libero_acis_stage1only_k07.yaml\"\n46\t\n47\tSUITE = \"libero_object\"\n48\tMAX_STEPS = 280\n49\tACTION_HORIZON = 50\n50\tACTION_DIM_NAMES = [\"pos_x\", \"pos_y\", \"pos_z\", \"rot_x\", \"rot_y\", \"rot_z\", \"gripper\"]\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef parse_args():\n54\t    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n55\t    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=2)\n56\t    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=5)\n57\t    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=\"0,1,2\")\n58\t    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n59\t    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, default=\"artifacts\")\n60\t    return p.parse_args()\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef load_config(config_path: str) -> dict:\n64\t    import copy\n65\t    import yaml\n66\t    config_path = Path(config_path)\n67\t    with open(config_path) as f:\n68\t        config = yaml.safe_load(f)\n69\t    if \"_base_\" in config:\n70\t        base_path = config_path.parent / config.pop(\"_base_\")\n71\t        base_config = load_config(str(base_path))\n72\t        for k, v in config.items():\n73\t            if k in base_config and isinstance(base_config[k], dict) and isinstance(v, dict):\n74\t                base_config[k].update(v)\n75\t            else:\n76\t                base_config[k] = v\n77\t        return base_config\n78\t    return config\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef load_baseline_model(device):\n82\t    from train import load_smolvla_policy\n83\t    config = load_config(BASELINE_CONFIG)\n84\t    policy = load_smolvla_policy(config, device)\n85\t    ckpt = torch.load(BASELINE_CKPT, map_location=device, weights_only=False)\n86\t    policy.load_state_dict(ckpt[\"model_state_dict\"], strict=False)\n87\t    policy.eval()\n88\t    return policy, config\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tdef load_acis_model(device):\n92\t    from train import load_smolvla_policy, wrap_with_imm\n93\t    config = load_config(ACIS_CONFIG)\n94\t    policy = load_smolvla_policy(config, device)\n95\t    wrapper = wrap_with_imm(policy, config)\n96\t    wrapper.to(device)\n97\t    ckpt = torch.load(ACIS_CKPT, map_location=device, weights_only=False)\n98\t    wrapper.load_state_dict(ckpt[\"model_state_dict\"], strict=False)\n99\t    wrapper.eval()\n100\t    return wrapper.policy, wrapper, config\n101\t\n102\t\n103\t# --- Observation / Action utilities (from eval_success_rate_libero.py) ---\n104\t\n105\tdef quat_to_axis_angle(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n106\t    q = quat.astype(np.float64)\n107\t    w = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\n108\t    den = np.sqrt(max(1.0 - w * w, 0.0))\n109\t    if den > 1e-10:\n110\t        angle = 2.0 * np.arccos(w)\n111\t        axis = q[:3] / den\n112\t        return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\n113\t    return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n114\t\n115\t\n116\tdef extract_state(obs: dict) -> np.ndarray:\n117\t    rs = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GQNbjk8Wx1yxVNsR8QoL3G","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Importance Margin Maximization losses.\n2\t\n3\tMarginMaximizationLoss: hinge loss  max(0, delta - (s_{(k)} - s_{(k+1)})).\n4\tIMMTotalLoss:           L_action + lambda * L_margin  with linear lambda warmup.\n5\t\n6\tInputs:  (B, N) importance scores, integer k.\n7\tDepends: soft_topk.SoftTopK.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\t\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport torch\n13\timport torch.nn as nn\n14\tfrom torch import Tensor\n15\tfrom typing import Dict, Tuple\n16\t\n17\tfrom .soft_topk import SoftTopK\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass MarginMaximizationLoss(nn.Module):\n21\t    \"\"\"Hinge loss: L_margin = mean(max(0, delta - (s_{(k)} - s_{(k+1)}))).\"\"\"\n22\t\n23\t    def __init__(self, delta: float = 1.0, temperature: float = 1.0, method: str = \"sigmoid\"):\n24\t        super().__init__()\n25\t        self.delta = delta\n26\t        self.soft_topk = SoftTopK(temperature=temperature, method=method)\n27\t\n28\t    def forward(self, importance_scores: Tensor, k: int) -> Tuple[Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\n29\t        \"\"\"\n30\t        Args:\n31\t            importance_scores: (B, N) per-token importance scores.\n32\t            k: number of tokens to retain.\n33\t        Returns:\n34\t            loss: scalar hinge loss.\n35\t            info: dict with margin, s_k, s_k_plus_1, mask for logging.\n36\t        \"\"\"\n37\t        mask, s_k, s_k_plus_1 = self.soft_topk(importance_scores, k)\n38\t\n39\t        margin = s_k - s_k_plus_1  # (B,)\n40\t        hinge = torch.clamp(self.delta - margin, min=0.0)  # (B,)\n41\t        loss = hinge.mean()\n42\t\n43\t        info = {\n44\t            \"margin\": margin.detach(),\n45\t            \"s_k\": s_k.detach(),\n46\t            \"s_k_plus_1\": s_k_plus_1.detach(),\n47\t            \"hinge\": hinge.detach(),\n48\t            \"mask\": mask.detach(),\n49\t        }\n50\t        return loss, info\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass IMMTotalLoss(nn.Module):\n54\t    \"\"\"L_total = L_action + lambda * L_margin, with linear lambda warmup.\"\"\"\n55\t\n56\t    def __init__(\n57\t        self,\n58\t        lambda_margin: float = 0.1,\n59\t        delta: float = 1.0,\n60\t        warmup_steps: int = 1000,\n61\t        temperature: float = 1.0,\n62\t        method: str = \"sigmoid\",\n63\t    ):\n64\t        super().__init__()\n65\t        self.lambda_margin = lambda_margin\n66\t        self.warmup_steps = warmup_steps\n67\t        self.margin_loss = MarginMaximizationLoss(delta=delta, temperature=temperature, method=method)\n68\t\n69\t    def _get_lambda(self, step: int) -> float:\n70\t        if self.warmup_steps <= 0:\n71\t            return self.lambda_margin\n72\t        return self.lambda_margin * min(1.0, step / self.warmup_steps)\n73\t\n74\t    def forward(\n75\t        self, action_loss: Tensor, importance_scores: Tensor, k: int, step: int\n76\t    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\n77\t        \"\"\"\n78\t        Args:\n79\t            action_loss: scalar task loss (e.g. action prediction).\n80\t            importance_scores: (B, N).\n81\t            k: retention count.\n82\t            step: current training step (for warmup).\n83\t        Returns:\n84\t            total_loss: scalar.\n85\t            info: dict with all sub-losses and diagnostics.\n86\t        \"\"\"\n87\t        lam = self._get_lambda(step)\n88\t        margin_loss, margin_info = self.margin_loss(importance_scores, k)\n89\t        total = action_loss + lam * margin_loss\n90\t\n91\t        info = {\n92\t            **margin_info,\n93\t            \"action_loss\": action_loss.detach(),\n94\t            \"margin_loss\": margin_loss.detach(),\n95\t            \"total_loss\": total.detach(),\n96\t            \"lambda\": torch.tensor(lam),\n97\t        }\n98\t        return total, info\n99\t\n100\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01XbF6stedgmyjhmPn1Xcp4k","session_id":"d75c42b7-1d15-4ab5-b75d-33f4c8f9f7de","uuid":"0c5f4570-0bf7-4b46-91b8-37313ab50b83","timestamp":"2026-05-20T05:59:03.039Z"}

n_visual = 64\n171\t    k_keep = int(n_visual * (1 - pruning_ratio))\n172\t\n173\t    def hook_fn(module, input, output):\n174\t        if isinstance(output, tuple):\n175\t            tokens = under:\n4\t  - Clean vs perturbed inputs (Gaussian noise eps in {0.01, 0.02, 0.05})\n5\t  - With/without ACIS-based 50% token pruning\n6\t  - Metrics: margin stats, top-k preservation rate, L*eps/Delta_k, Kendall tau, certified bound\n7\t\n8\tUsage:\n9\t    python evaluate.py --checkpoint path/to/ckpt --config configs/imm_anchor.yaml --gpu 0\n10\t    python evaluate.py --checkpoint path/to/baseline --config configs/baseline.yaml --output results/baseline.json\n11\t\n12\tDepends: lerobot, torch, imm.\n13\t\"\"\"\n14\t\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\timport argparse\n18\timport hashlib\n19\timport json\n20\timport logging\n21\timport sys\n22\tfrom pathlib import Path\n23\tfrom typing import Any, Dict, List\n24\t\n25\timport torch\n26\timport torch.nn.functional as F\n27\timport yaml\n28\t\n29\tARTIFACTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n30\tsys.path.insert(0, str(ARTIFACTS_DIR))\n31\t\n32\tfrom imm.metrics import (\n33\t    certified_bound,\n34\t    compute_empirical_lipschitz,\n35\t    compute_margin,\n36\t    kendall_tau_distance,\n37\t    l_eps_delta_ratio,\n38\t    margin_statistics,\n39\t    topk_preservation_rate,\n40\t)\n41\t\n42\tlogging.basicConfig(\n43\t    level=logging.INFO,\n44\t    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n45\t    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n46\t)\n47\tlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef parse_args():\n51\t    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"IMM evaluation for SmolVLA on LIBERO\")\n52\t    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str, required=True, help=\"Path to checkpoint file or directory\")\n53\t    parser.add_argument(\"--config\", type=str, required=True, help=\"Path to YAML config file\")\n54\t    parser.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0, help=\"GPU index\")\n55\t    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to save results JSON Any]:\n64\t    \"\"\"Load YAML config with _base_ inheritance.\"\"\"\n65\t    import copy\n66\t    config_path = Path(config_path)\n67\t    with open(config_path) as f:\n68\t        config = model.load_state_dict(state[\"model_state_dict\"], Implements ACIS (Attention-based Importance Score) pruning for OpenVLA models\n  - Masks/drops vision tokens at different keep ratios (0.1 to 1.0)\n  - Measures success rate on LIBERO tasks (280 max steps)\n  - Two-pass inference: 1) scoring pass with attention output, 2) pruning + generation pass\n\n**X-VLA Pruning Evaluation:**\n- `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py` (377 lines)\n  - Prunes vision tokens by L2 norm after Florence2 encoder\n  - Takes `--keep_ratio` parameter (default 0.5)\n  - Evaluates on LIBERO-Object with 50 episodes per task\n\n**Pruning Sweep Shell Script:**\n- `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/run_xvla_prune_sweep.sh`\n  - Sweeps keep_ratio values: [1.0, 0.95, 0.9, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1]\n  - Collects results in JSON format with keep_ratio as key\n  - Produces summary table with tokens kept, success rates, and CI bounds\n\n### 2. **Action Loss Computation Modules**\n\n**IMM Loss Module:**\n- `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/imm/losses.py`\n  - `IMMTotalLoss`: Combines action loss + lambda * margin loss\n  - Computes `action_loss` via MSE: `F.mse_loss(predicted_actions, target_actions)`\n  - Returns per-batch action loss and margin statistics\n\n**SmolVLA Wrapper:**\n- `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/imm/smolvla_wrapper.py`\n  - `action_loss = F.mse_loss(...)` on dimension [0:action_dim]\n  - Passes action_loss to `imm_loss` for margin loss computation\n  - Returns normalized action loss in info dict\n\n**Action Importance Analysis:**\n- `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/eval_token_importance.py` (479 lines)\n  - Computes gradient magnitude of action loss w.r.t. each visual token\n  - Correlates ACIS scores with gradient-based importance\n\n### 3. **Attention-based Token Importance Scoring (ACIS)**\n\n**ACIS Module:**\n- `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/imm/acis.py`\n  - Cross-attention scorer: action_query attends to visual_tokens\n  - Outputs per-token importance scores: `(B, N)`\n  - All linear layers wrapped with spectral normalization for Lipschitz control\n  - Combines learned scores with attention-based importance: `score_logits + token_importance`\n\n**Key computation in eval_openvla_acis_prune.py:**\n  - Line 220: `importance = compute_attention_importance(outputs.attentions, N_vis, attn_agg)`\n  - Line 222: `k = max(1, int(N_vis * keep_ratio))`\n  - Line 223: `_, topk_indices = torch.topk(importance, k, dim=-1)` - selects top-k tokens\n\n### 4. **Existing Pruning Curve JSON Results**\n\n**Complete Pruning Sweep Data:**\n\n1. **pruning_sweep_v2.json** (3.3 KB)\n   - Location: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/eval_results/pruning_sweep_v2.json`\n   - k_ratios: [0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9]\n   - losses_pruned: Action loss at each k (normalized)\n   - losses_baseline: 0.0181 (std=0.0207)\n   - G3 values: Certified robustness metric at each k\n   - Sweet spot: k_ratio=0.6 (G3=0.2374)\n   - Deployable range: [0.3, 0.9]\n   - Per-ratio details include: margin_mean, margin_std, margin_median, status (PASS/FAIL)\n   - 500 batches evaluated, seed=42\n\n2. **pruning_sweep.json** (3.2 KB)\n   - Single batch version for quick testing\n   - Same structure but with 1 batch of evaluation data\n   - Sweet spot: k_ratio=0.5\n\n### 5. **Normalized Action Loss Computation**\n\n**Loss normalization approach (from pruning_sweep_v2.json):**\n- For each keep_ratio k:\n  1. Compute MSE action loss on all batches: `F.mse_loss(pred_actions, target_actions)`\n  2. Average across all samples: `mean(action_loss_per_batch)`\n  3. Report as \"loss_pruned\" in per_ratio_details\n  4. Baseline loss computed from unprune model: 0.0181\n\n**Normalization context in logs:**\n  - Baseline loss: 0.018181 (computed from 500 batches)\n  - Loss at k=0.5: 0.004373 (76% reduction!)\n  - Loss at k=0.6: 0.004315 (76% reduction, sweet spot)\n  - Loss at k=0.3: 0.017068 (6% reduction)\n\n### 6. **Pruning Curve Analysis Structure**\n\nThe `per_ratio_details` dictionary contains for each keep_ratio k:\n```json\n{\n  \"g3\": &lt;certified_robustness_metric>,\n  \"loss_pruned\": &lt;action_mse_loss>,\n  \"loss_pruned_std\": &lt;standard_deviation>,\n  \"loss_baseline\": &lt;baseline_loss>,\n  \"margin_mean\": &lt;margin_metric>,\n  \"margin_median\": &lt;median_margin>,\n  \"margin_std\": &lt;margin_std>,\n  \"status\": \"PASS\" or \"FAIL\"\n}\n```\n\nThe G3 metric indicates:\n- G3 < 1.0: Denoising effect (robustness improvement)\n- G3 = sweet_spot (k=0.6): Optimal balance\n- Values shown for each k demonstrate robustness-efficiency tradeoff\n\nAll files are in artifacts/ directory with results in `eval_results/pruning_sweep*.json` format."}]}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d75c42b7-1d15-4ab5-b75d-33f4c8f9f7de","uuid":"44dce86e-97f6-4695-b266-03dbfcdf4926","timestamp":"2026-05-20T06:00:36.791Z","tool_use_result":{"status":"completed","prompt":"In /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/, find scripts that compute pruning curves (loss-based vision token pruning). moojink/openvla-oft: Fine-Tuning Vision-Language-Action Models: Optimizing Speed and Success · GitHub\",\"url\":\"https://github.com/moojink/openvla-oft\"},{\"title\":\"moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-10 · Hugging Face\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-10\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's information about OpenVLA-OFT model weights and how to download them:\n\n## Overview\n\nOpenVLA-OFT sets a new state of the art on the LIBERO simulation benchmark, significantly boosting OpenVLA's average success rate across four task suites from 76.5% to 97.1% while increasing action generation throughput by 26 times.\n\n## Model Weights Availability\n\nCode for OFT and pretrained model checkpoints are released at https://openvla-oft.github.io/.\n\nAdditionally, OpenVLA-OFT weights after mix-SFT can be downloaded from HuggingFace repositories. There are several fine-tuned OpenVLA-OFT checkpoints available on HuggingFace:\n\n- `moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-spatial`\n- `moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-object`\n- `moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-10`\n- `Sylvest/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-plus-mixdata`\n\n## Usage\n\nYou can download a pretrained OpenVLA-OFT checkpoint and run inference to generate an action chunk using Python code that specifies the checkpoint path (e.g., \"moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-spatial\").\n\nThe official GitHub repository at https://github.com/moojink/openvla-oft contains the implementation and usage instructions for OpenVLA-OFT.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d75c42b7-1d15-4ab5-b75d-33f4c8f9f7de","uuid":"e78e714d-1bc1-4e62-8800-d27bb5e54396","timestamp":"2026-05-20T06:01:01.984Z","tool_use_result":{"query":"OpenVLA-OFT huggingface model weights download \"openvla-oft\"","results":[{"tool_use_id":"srvtoolu_01E7qrKQbSBhjwLcTaHfofBT","content":[{"title":"GitHub - How to Load This Model\n\n### Using Transformers Library:\n```python\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq\nmodel = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\"moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-object\", trust_remote_code=True, dtype=\"auto\")\n```\n\n### Complete Quick Start Example:\n```python\nimport pickle\nfrom experiments.robot.libero.run_libero_eval import GenerateConfig\nfrom experiments.robot.openvla_utils import get_action_head, get_processor, get_proprio_projector, get_vla, get_vla_action\nfrom prismatic.vla.constants import NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK, PROPRIO_DIM\n\ncfg = GenerateConfig(\n    pretrained_checkpoint = \"moojink/openvla-7b-oft-finetuned-libero-object\",\n    use_l1_regression = True,\n    use_diffusion = False,\n    use_film = False,\n    num_images_in_input = 2,\n    use_proprio = True,\n    load_in_8bit = False,\n    load_in_4bit = False,\n    center_crop = True,\n    num_open_loop_steps = NUM_ACTIONS_CHUNK,\n    unnorm_key = \"libero_object_no_noops\",\n)\n\nvla = get_vla(cfg)\nprocessor = get_processor(cfg)\naction_head = get_action_head(cfg, llm_dim=vla.llm_dim)\nproprio_projector = get_proprio_projector(cfg, llm_dim=vla.llm_dim, proprio_dim=PROPRIO_DIM)\n\nwith open(\"experiments/robot/libero/sample_libero_object_observation.pkl\", \"rb\") as file:\n    observation = pickle.load(file)\n\nactions = get_vla_action(cfg, vla, processor, observation, observation[\"task_description\"], action_head, proprio_projector)\n```\n\n## 2) Architecture Details\n\n- **Action Head Type**: MLP action head for L1 regression (generates continuous actions)\n- **Connectors**: \n  - Proprio projector (maps proprioceptive state to language embedding space)\n  - Vision-language-action integration\n\n## 3) Repository Files\n\n- **Model Size**: 8B parameters\n- **Tensor Type**: BF16\n- **Format**: Safetensors\n- **Tags**: Robotics, Transformers, openvla, feature-extraction, custom_code\n\n## 4) Special Dependencies\n\n- **openvla-oft repository**: https://github.com/openvla-oft/openvla-oft (required for full setup)\n- Conda environment setup as described in GitHub README\n- Libraries: `experiments.robot.libero`, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01FfMV1RA18oMs38sBqwPKiY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The download was kicked off in the background with PID 13653. Now let me:\n1. Write the analysis script while waiting for the download\n2. Check download progress periodically\n\nLet me first understand the OFT model structure by checking the model config on HuggingFace. The key question is whether OFT's VLM backbone has the same architecture as OpenVLA (Llama-2 7B with Prismatic vision encoder), just with different weights.\n\nLooking at the HuggingFace card, it said `AutoModelForVision2Seq` with trust_remote_code. \n\nI'll check the config.json from the downloaded files to see the actual architecture details, then adapt my existing analysis script for OFT. Actually, it's already retrying automatically - I'll just let it run and move on to writing the analysis script in the meantime.\n\nSince the OFT model uses the same architecture and config structure as OpenVLA, I can load it with the same Prismatic code from the openvla-libero-object directory, just pointing to a different checkpoint. For the analysis, I need to build an entropy script and a pruning curve that loads the model, extracts vision token embeddings after the projector, sorts them by L2 norm, then iteratively masks lower-norm tokens and measures the action loss across different retention percentages. The action head uses 256 bins to tokenize actions, so I need to measure cross-entropy on those action token positions.\n\nBut I'm noticing a potential discrepancy—the model card mentions an MLP action head for L1 regression, which would be different from the tokenized approach. Looking at the auto_map in the config, the OFT checkpoint has its own modeling_prismatic.py that might implement continuous action prediction instead of tokenization, even though the config still lists n_action_bins=256 from the base class.\n\nThe key insight is that OFT loads the action head separately via `get_action_head(cfg, llm_dim=vla.llm_dim)`, meaning it's not part of the VLM checkpoint itself—the VLM just produces hidden states that feed into a separate MLP. For entropy analysis, I can load with trust_remote_code=True and output_attentions=True since that only depends on the backbone. For the pruning curve, since the action head isn't in the checkpoint, I'll measure representation degradation instead by comparing the last hidden states between full and pruned forward passes using MSE loss.\n\nBut actually, looking back at the original scripts, `measure_openvla_entropy_real.py` only does entropy analysis while `eval_openvla_acis_prune.py` computes the pruning curve in a simulation environment. For an offline pruning curve, I need to think through the approach differently: forward the model with full vision tokens to get the baseline output, rank vision tokens by L2 norm, then for each keep ratio, forward with only the top-k tokens and measure the loss between full and pruned outputs.\n\nThe loss metric matters for fair comparison—hidden state degradation (MSE between full and pruned hidden states) is most appropriate since OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT have different action heads. But the user asked for \"normalized action loss,\" and looking at the pruning_sweep_v2.json results, it appears to be MSE action loss rather than cross-entropy. For the OFT model, since the VLM backbone produces hidden states feeding into an L1 regression head, I need to figure out how to compute a comparable metric.\n\nWriting evaluation script... 2. Rank by L2 norm\n3. For each k: mask the bottom (1-k) tokens\n4. Forward through LLM\n5. Measure output degradation\n\nI can implement this either by hooking into the model to intercept vision embeddings, modifying the forward pass directly, or running the projector separately and then the LLM. Looking at the Prismatic architecture, I see the vision backbone, projector, and language model are separate components, so I'll use a hook-based approach—registering one on the projector to capture the vision embeddings, compute their L2 norms for ranking, and then selectively mask tokens during the forward pass.\n\nActually, the cleaner approach is to use `output_hidden_states=True` to get embeddings at each layer, then manually construct `inputs_embeds` with masked vision tokens for pruning. The model pipeline works by encoding pixels through the vision encoder, projecting those features, and then inserting them into the token embedding space at the right positions. for Monkey-Patching ===\\\\ndef unpack_tuple(fn: Callable[[Any], Tuple[Any]]) -> Callable[[Any], Any]:\\\\n    def wrapper(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:\\\\n        result = fn(*args, **kwargs)\\\\n        return result[0] if isinstance(result, (tuple, list)) else result\\\\n\\\\n    return wrapper\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# HF Transformers overwrites parameters with names containing `gamma`; we're going to patch VisionBackbone.LayerScale.\\\\n#   =>> TIMM :: https://github.com/huggingface/pytorch-image-models/blob/main/timm/models/vision_transformer.py#L109\\\\n#   =>> Transformers :: https://github.com/huggingface/transformers/blob/main/src/transformers/modeling_utils.py#L3960\\\\ndef _ls_new_forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n    return x.mul_(self.scale_factor) if self.inplace else x * self.scale_factor\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef ls_apply_patch(ls_module: LayerScale):\\\\n    ls_module.scale_factor = nn.Parameter(ls_module.gamma.clone())\\\\n    ls_module.forward = _ls_new_forward.__get__(ls_module, LayerScale)\\\\n    del ls_module.gamma\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# === Prismatic Vision Backbone (nn.Module) Definitions (w/ Fused Backbone Support) ===\\\\nclass PrismaticVisionBackbone(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        use_fused_vision_backbone: bool,\\\\n        image_sizes: List[int],\\\\n        timm_model_ids: List[str],\\\\n        timm_override_act_layers: List[Optional[str]],\\\\n    ) -> None:\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.use_fused_vision_backbone = use_fused_vision_backbone\\\\n\\\\n        # [Contract] Validate number of (fused) vision backbones, create \\\\\\\"alpha\\\\\\\" featurizer and Instantiate\\\\n        #   =>> Note :: Monkey-Patch the `forward()` function of the backbone to ensure FSDP-compatibility\\\\n        #               Hardcodes `get_intermediate_layers` to return the **SECOND-TO-LAST** layer patches!\\\\n        assert len(timm_model_ids) <= 2, \\\\\\\"Prismatic models only support up to 2 (fused) vision backbones!\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.featurizer = timm.create_model(\\\\n            timm_model_ids[0],\\\\n            pretrained=False,\\\\n            num_classes=0,\\\\n            img_size=image_sizes[0],\\\\n            act_layer=timm_override_act_layers[0],\\\\n        )\\\\n        self.featurizer.forward = unpack_tuple(\\\\n            partial(self.featurizer.get_intermediate_layers, n={len(self.featurizer.blocks) - 2})\\\\n        )\\\\n        self.embed_dim = self.featurizer.embed_dim\\\\n\\\\n        # If `use_fused_vision_backbone` =>> create \\\\\\\"beta\\\\\\\" featurizer\\\\n        if self.use_fused_vision_backbone:\\\\n            self.fused_featurizer = timm.create_model(\\\\n                timm_model_ids[1],\\\\n                pretrained=False,\\\\n                num_classes=0,\\\\n                img_size=image_sizes[1],\\\\n                act_layer=timm_override_act_layers[1],\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 763 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\ntorch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run image (`pixel_values`) through featurizer; if channel-stacked, then dispatch and sequence stack.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if not self.use_fused_vision_backbone:\\\\n            return self.featurizer(pixel_values)\\\\n\\\\n        # Split `pixel_values :: [bsz, 2 * 3, resolution, resolution]` =>> featurize =>> channel stack\\\\n        img, img_fused = torch.split(pixel_values, [3, 3], dim=1)\\\\n        patches, patches_fused = self.featurizer(img), self.fused_featurizer(img_fused)\\\\n\\\\n        return torch.cat([patches, patches_fused], dim=2)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# === Prismatic Projector (nn.Module) Definitions ===\\\\nclass PrismaticProjector(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, use_fused_vision_backbone: bool, vision_dim: int, llm_dim: int) -> None:\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.use_fused_vision_backbone = use_fused_vision_backbone\\\\n        self.vision_dim, self.llm_dim = vision_dim, llm_dim\\\\n\\\\n        # Switch on `use_fused_vision_backbone` =>> use slightly different MLPs and projection factors!\\\\n        if not self.use_fused_vision_backbone:\\\\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(self.vision_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(self.llm_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\n            self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\\\n        else:\\\\n            initial_projection_dim = 4 * vision_dim\\\\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(self.vision_dim, initial_projection_dim, bias=True)\\\\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(initial_projection_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(self.llm_dim, self.llm_dim, bias=True)\\\\n            self.act_fn1 = nn.GELU()\\\\n            self.act_fn2 = nn.GELU()\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, img_patches: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        if not self.use_fused_vision_backbone:\\\\n            projected_features = self.fc1(img_patches)\\\\n            projected_features = self.act_fn1(projected_features)\\\\n            projected_features = self.fc2(projected_features)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            projected_features = self.fc1(img_patches)\\\\n            projected_features = self.act_fn1(projected_features)\\\\n            projected_features = self.fc2(projected_features)\\\\n            projected_features = self.act_fn2(projected_features)\\\\n            projected_features = self.fc3(projected_features)\\\\n\\\\n        return projected_features\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# === Main HF Class Definitions ===\\\\n@dataclass\\\\nclass PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast(ModelOutput):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Base class for PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run a forward pass through the VLM, returning a PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast instance.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        output_attentions = output_attentions if output_attentions is not None else self.config.output_attentions\\n        output_hidden_states = (\\n            output_hidden_states if output_hidden_states is not None else return_dict is not None else self.config.use_return_dict\\n\\n        # Respect `use_cache` only if not training (even if `gradient_checkpointing` is off)\\n        use_cache = use_cache and not self.training\\n\\n        # Instantiate Placeholder for Projector Features\\n        projected_patch_embeddings = None\\n\\n        # Note :: We only support forward passes with the following cases:\\n        #   => Cached Generation :: (input_ids.shape[1] == 1) and (past_key_values is not None)\\n        #   => Unimodal Forward :: (pixel_values is None)\\n        #   => Multimodal Forward :: (pixel_values is not None) and (input_ids/embeds.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0])\\n\\n        # === Handle Generation with Cache (`input_ids.shape[1] == 1`) =>> requires `past_keys_values` ===\\n        if input_ids.shape[1] == 1:\\n            assert input_ids.shape[0] == 1, \\\"Generation is only currently supported for batch size of 1!\\\"\\n            assert past_key_values is not None, \\\"You must provide `past_key_values` during cached generation!\\\"\\n            assert labels is None, \\\"Unexpected key `labels` provided during cached generation!\\\"\\n\\n            language_model_output = self.language_model(\\n                input_ids=input_ids,\\n                attention_mask=None,\\n                position_ids=None,\\n                past_key_values=past_key_values,\\n                inputs_embeds=None,\\n                labels=None,\\n                use_cache=use_cache,\\n                output_attentions=output_attentions,\\n                output_hidden_states=output_hidden_states,\\n                return_dict=return_dict,\\n            )\\n\\n        # === Handle Unimodal Forward ===\\n        elif pixel_values is None:\\n            assert (input_ids is not None) and (inputs_embeds is None), \\\"Missing `input_ids` in language-only forward!\\\"\\n            assert past_key_values is None, \\\"Unexpected key `past_key_values` provided during language-only forward!\\\"\\n\\n            language_model_output = self.language_model(\\n                input_ids=input_ids,\\n                attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n                position_ids=None,\\n                past_key_values=None,\\n                inputs_embeds=None,\\n                labels=labels,\\n                use_cache=use_cache,\\n                output_attentions=output_attentions,\\n                output_hidden_states=output_hidden_states,\\n                return_dict=return_dict,\\n            )\\n\\n        # === Handle Multimodal Forward ===\\n        elif (input_ids.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0]) or (inputs_embeds.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0]):\\n            assert past_key_values is None, \\\"Unexpected key `past_key_values` provided during language-only forward!\\\"\\n\\n            # Visual Feature Extraction\\n            patch_features = self.vision_backbone(pixel_values)\\n\\n            # Projectio\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 5423 字符] ...\\n\\nel_values,\\n                \\\"past_key_values\\\": past_key_values,\\n                \\\"use_cache\\\": kwargs.get(\\\"use_cache\\\"),\\n            }\\n        )\\n\\n        return model_inputs\\n\\n    # Defer to Language Model (all handle this differently, with different return types)\\n    def _reorder_cache(self, *args, **kwargs) -> Any:\\n        return self.language_model._reorder_cache(*args, **kwargs)\\n\\n\\nclass OpenVLAForActionPrediction(PrismaticForConditionalGeneration):\\n    config_class: PretrainedConfig = OpenVLAConfig\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: OpenVLAConfig) -> None:\\n        super().__init__(config)\\n        self.norm_stats = config.norm_stats\\n\\n        # Compute action bins\\n        self.bins = np.linspace(-1, 1, config.n_action_bins)\\n        self.bin_centers = (self.bins[:-1] + self.bins[1:]) / 2.0\\n\\n        # Compute vocab size for de-tokenization -- revert added \\\"multiple of\\\"\\n        self.vocab_size = self.config.text_config.vocab_size - self.config.pad_to_multiple_of\\n\\n    def predict_action(\\n        self, input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None, **kwargs: str\\n    ) -> np.ndarray:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Thin wrapper around .generate() that decodes predicted actions and unnormalizes them.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # If the special empty token ('') does not already appear after the colon (':') token in the prompt\\n        # (after \\\"OUT:\\\" or \\\"ASSISTANT:\\\"), insert it to match the inputs seen at training time\\n        if not torch.all(input_ids[:, -1] == 29871):\\n            input_ids = torch.cat(\\n                (input_ids, torch.unsqueeze(torch.Tensor([29871]).long(), dim=0).to(input_ids.device)), dim=1\\n            )\\n\\n        # Run VLA inference\\n        generated_ids = self.generate(input_ids, max_new_tokens=self.get_action_dim(unnorm_key), **kwargs)\\n\\n        # Extract predicted action tokens and translate into (normalized) continuous actions\\n        predicted_action_token_ids = generated_ids[0, -self.get_action_dim(unnorm_key) :].cpu().numpy()\\n        discretized_actions = self.vocab_size - predicted_action_token_ids\\n        discretized_actions = np.clip(discretized_actions - 1, a_min=0, a_max=self.bin_centers.shape[0] - 1)\\n        normalized_actions = self.bin_centers[discretized_actions]\\n\\n        # Unnormalize actions\\n        action_norm_stats = self.get_action_stats(unnorm_key)\\n        mask = action_norm_stats.get(\\\"mask\\\", np.ones_like(action_norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"], dtype=bool))\\n        action_high, action_low = np.array(action_norm_stats[\\\"q99\\\"]), np.array(action_norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"])\\n        actions = np.where(\\n            mask,\\n            0.5 * (normalized_actions + 1) * (action_high - action_low) + action_low,\\n            normalized_actions,\\n        )\\n\\n        return actions\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _check_unnorm_key(norm_stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], unnorm_key: Optional[str]) -> str:\\n        if unnorm_key is None:\\n            assert len(norm_stats) == 1, (\\n                f\\\"Your model was trained on more than one dataset, \\\"\\n                f\\\"please pass a `unnorm_key` from the following options to choose the statistics \\\"\\n                f\\\"used for un-normalizing actions: {norm_stats.keys()}\\\"\\n            )\\n            unnorm_key = next(iter(norm_stats.keys()))\\n\\n        assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, (\\n            f\\\"The `unnorm_key` you chose is not in the set of available dataset statistics, \\\"\\n            f\\\"please choose from: {norm_stats.keys()}\\\"\\n        )\\n        return unnorm_key\\n\\n    def get_action_dim(self, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Get the dimensionality of the policy's action space.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unnorm_key = self._check_unnorm_key(self.norm_stats, unnorm_key)\\n        return len(self.norm_stats[unnorm_key][\\\"action\\\"][\\\"q01\\\"])\\n\\n    def get_action_stats(self, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Get all the logged statistics for the given dataset.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unnorm_key = self._check_unnorm_key(self.norm_stats, unnorm_key)\\n        return self.norm_stats[unnorm_key][\\\"action\\\"]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.94}}"}}}

Measures:\n  A) Pruning curve: L2-norm based vision token pruning → hidden state degradation\n  B) Attention entropy per layer (text→vision attention distribution)\n\nUsage:\n    source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python analyze_openvla_oft.py\n\"\"\"\n\nimport glob\nimport json\nimport math\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\n\nimport h5py\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nDEVICE = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\nCHECKPOINT_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-oft-libero-object\"\nLIBERO_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/libero_data/datasets/libero_object\"\nN_VISION_TOKENS = 256\nIMAGES_PER_TASK = 10\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts\"\n\nKEEP_RATIOS = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0]\n\nTASK_PROMPTS = {\n    \"pick_up_the_alphabet_soup\": \"pick up the alphabet soup and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_cream_cheese\": \"pick up the cream cheese and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_butter\": \"pick up the butter and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_ketchup\": \"pick up the ketchup and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_tomato_sauce\": \"pick up the tomato sauce and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_bbq_sauce\": \"pick up the bbq sauce and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_chocolate_pudding\": \"pick up the chocolate pudding and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_salad_dressing\": \"pick up the salad dressing and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_milk\": \"pick up the milk and place it in the basket\",\n    \"pick_up_the_orange_juice\": \"pick up the orange juice and place it in the basket\",\n}\nDEFAULT_PROMPT = \"pick up the object and place it in the basket\"\n\n\ndef get_prompt_for_task(task_name):\n    tn_lower = task_name.lower()\n    for key, desc in TASK_PROMPTS.items():\n        if key in tn_lower:\n            return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {desc}?\\nOut:\"\n    return f\"In: What action should the robot take to {DEFAULT_PROMPT}?\\nOut:\"\n\n\ndef load_libero_images():\n    hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"*.hdf5\")))\n    if not hdf5_files:\n        hdf5_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LIBERO_DIR, \"**/*.hdf5\"), recursive=True))\n    print(f\"[*] Found {len(hdf5_files)} HDF5 files in {LIBERO_DIR}\")\n\n    all_images = []\n    task_names = []\n\n    for hdf5_path in hdf5_files:\n        task_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(hdf5_path))[0]\n\n        with h5py.File(hdf5_path, \"r\") as f:\n            if \"data\" not in f:\n                continue\n            demo_keys = sorted(\n                [k for k in f[\"data\"].keys() if k.startswith(\"demo\")],\n                key=lambda x: int(x.split(\"_\")[-1])\n            )\n            if not demo_keys:\n                continue\n\n            obs_keys = list(f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs\"].keys())\n            img_key = None\n            for candidate in [\"agentview_rgb\", \"agentview_image\", \"image\", \"rgb\"]:\n                if candidate in obs_keys:\n                    img_key = candidate\n                    break\n            if img_key is None:\n                for k in obs_keys:\n                    shape = f[f\"data/{demo_keys[0]}/obs/{k}\"].shape\n                    if len(shape) == 4 and shape[-1] == 3:\n                        img_key = k\n                        break\n            if img_key is None:\n                continue\n\n            frame_pool = []\n            for dk in demo_keys:\n                n_frames = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"].shape[0]\n                frame_pool.append((dk, n_frames))\n            total_frames = sum(n for _, n in frame_pool)\n\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n            target = min(IMAGES_PER_TASK, total_frames)\n            indices = []\n            for dk, n in frame_pool:\n                for i in range(n):\n                    indices.append((dk, i))\n            chosen = rng.choice(len(indices), size=target, replace=False)\n            chosen.sort()\n\n            by_demo = {}\n            for idx in chosen:\n                dk, frame_i = indices[idx]\n                by_demo.setdefault(dk, []).append(frame_i)\n\n            sampled = 0\n            for dk, frame_indices in by_demo.items():\n                imgs = f[f\"data/{dk}/obs/{img_key}\"][:]\n                for fi in frame_indices:\n                    all_images.append(Image.fromarray(imgs[fi]))\n                    task_names.append(task_name)\n                    sampled += 1\n\n            print(f\"  {task_name}: {sampled} frames\")\n\n    unique_tasks = sorted(set(task_names))\n    print(f\"[*] Total: {len(all_images)} images from {len(unique_tasks)} tasks\")\n    return all_images, task_names, unique_tasks\n\n\ndef load_model():\n    print(f\"\\n[*] Loading OpenVLA-OFT from: {CHECKPOINT_PATH}\", flush=True)\n\n    # Add the checkpoint dir to path so trust_remote_code can find the modules\n    sys.path.insert(0, CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoConfig\n\n    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        CHECKPOINT_PATH,\n        config=config,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n        attn_implementation=\"eager\",\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n    ).to(DEVICE)\n    model.eval()\n\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n\n    # Load processor components\n    proc_module = __import__(\"processing_prismatic\", fromlist=[\"PrismaticImageProcessor\", \"PrismaticProcessor\"])\n    PrismaticImageProcessor = proc_module.PrismaticImageProcessor\n    PrismaticProcessor = proc_module.PrismaticProcessor\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    image_processor = PrismaticImageProcessor.from_pretrained(CHECKPOINT_PATH)\n    processor = PrismaticProcessor(image_processor=image_processor, tokenizer=tokenizer)\n\n    print(f\"[*] Model loaded, GPU mem: {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f} GB\", flush=True)\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_bits(p, eps=1e-10):\n    p = p.clamp(min=eps)\n    return -(p * p.log2()).sum(dim=-1)\n\n\nclass ProjectorHook:\n    \"\"\"Hook to capture and optionally prune projector output.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        self.projected_features = None\n        self.norms = None\n\n    def __call__(self, module, input, output):\n        self.projected_features = output.detach().clone()\n        self.norms = output.detach().norm(dim=-1)  # (B, N)\n\n        if self.keep_ratio < 1.0:\n            B, N, D = output.shape\n            k = max(1, int(N * self.keep_ratio))\n            _, topk_idx = torch.topk(self.norms, k, dim=-1)\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=output.device, dtype=output.dtype)\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\n            output = output * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks):\n    n_images = len(images)\n    n_layers = model.language_model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    n_heads = model.language_model.config.num_attention_heads\n    print(f\"[*] LLM: {n_layers} layers, {n_heads} heads\", flush=True)\n\n    # Register projector hook\n    hook = ProjectorHook()\n    handle = model.projector.register_forward_hook(hook)\n\n    # === Part A: Attention Entropy ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part A: Attention Entropy Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_entropies = []\n    all_vision_attn_frac = []\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_attentions=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n\n        attentions = outputs.attentions\n\n        if idx == 0:\n            seq_len = attentions[0].shape[-1]\n            text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n            print(f\"[*] Seq len: {seq_len}, vision: [1, {1+N_VISION_TOKENS}), text starts: {text_start}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Attention shape: {attentions[0].shape}\")\n            print(f\"[*] Layers returned: {len(attentions)}\", flush=True)\n\n        text_start = 1 + N_VISION_TOKENS\n        sample_entropies = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n        sample_vis_frac = np.zeros((n_layers, n_heads))\n\n        for layer_idx in range(len(attentions)):\n            attn = attentions[layer_idx]\n            text_to_vision = attn[0, :, text_start:, 1:1+N_VISION_TOKENS].float()\n            text_to_all = attn[0, :, text_start:, :].float()\n\n            vis_frac = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1) / text_to_all.sum(dim=-1).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            sample_vis_frac[layer_idx] = vis_frac.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n            vis_sum = text_to_vision.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-10)\n            text_to_vision_norm = text_to_vision / vis_sum\n\n            ent = compute_entropy_bits(text_to_vision_norm)\n            sample_entropies[layer_idx] = ent.mean(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n\n        all_entropies.append(sample_entropies)\n        all_vision_attn_frac.append(sample_vis_frac)\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            cur_mean = np.mean(all_entropies)\n            cur_ratio = cur_mean / math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] entropy_ratio={cur_ratio:.4f}\", flush=True)\n\n        del outputs, attentions\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    all_entropies = np.array(all_entropies)\n    all_vision_attn_frac = np.array(all_vision_attn_frac)\n    max_entropy = math.log2(N_VISION_TOKENS)\n\n    per_layer_mean = all_entropies.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_std = all_entropies.std(axis=(0, 2))\n    per_layer_ratio = per_layer_mean / max_entropy\n    per_layer_ratio_std = per_layer_std / max_entropy\n    per_layer_vis_frac = all_vision_attn_frac.mean(axis=(0, 2))\n\n    overall_mean = float(all_entropies.mean())\n    overall_std = float(all_entropies.std())\n    overall_ratio = overall_mean / max_entropy\n    overall_ratio_std = overall_std / max_entropy\n\n    min_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmin())\n    max_layer = int(per_layer_ratio.argmax())\n\n    print(f\"\\n[ENTROPY RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"Overall entropy ratio: {overall_ratio:.4f} +/- {overall_ratio_std:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Min layer: {min_layer} (ratio={per_layer_ratio[min_layer]:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"Max layer: {max_layer} (ratio={per_layer_ratio[max_layer]:.4f})\")\n\n    entropy_results = {\n        \"model\": \"OpenVLA-OFT-7B-libero-object\",\n        \"dataset\": \"LIBERO-Object\",\n        \"n_images\": n_images,\n        \"n_tasks_covered\": len(unique_tasks),\n        \"tasks\": unique_tasks,\n        \"n_vision_tokens\": N_VISION_TOKENS,\n        \"max_entropy_bits\": max_entropy,\n        \"n_layers\": n_layers,\n        \"n_heads\": n_heads,\n        \"per_layer_entropy_ratio\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio))},\n        \"per_layer_entropy_std\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio_std[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio_std))},\n        \"per_layer_vision_attention_fraction\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_vis_frac[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_vis_frac))},\n        \"overall_entropy_ratio\": round(overall_ratio, 4),\n        \"overall_entropy_std\": round(overall_ratio_std, 4),\n        \"overall_vision_attention_fraction\": round(float(all_vision_attn_frac.mean()), 4),\n        \"min_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": min_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[min_layer]), 4)},\n        \"max_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": max_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[max_layer]), 4)},\n    }\n\n    # === Part B: Pruning Curve ===\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part B: Pruning Curve (L2-norm, hidden state degradation)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_losses = {k: [] for k in KEEP_RATIOS}\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        # Full forward (k=1.0) to get reference hidden states\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_full = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_hidden_states=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n        h_full = out_full.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()  # text positions only\n        h_full_norm_sq = (h_full ** 2).sum().item()\n\n        pruning_losses[1.0].append(0.0)\n\n        for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n            if k >= 1.0:\n                continue\n            hook.keep_ratio = k\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                out_pruned = model(\n                    **inputs,\n                    output_hidden_states=True,\n                    return_dict=True,\n                )\n            h_pruned = out_pruned.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()\n            degradation = ((h_pruned - h_full) ** 2).sum().item()\n            normalized = degradation / max(h_full_norm_sq, 1e-10)\n            pruning_losses[k].append(normalized)\n\n            del out_pruned\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        del out_full\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            summary = {k: f\"{np.mean(v):.6f}\" for k, v in pruning_losses.items() if v}\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] losses={summary}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_results = {}\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        vals = pruning_losses[k]\n        pruning_results[str(k)] = {\n            \"normalized_loss\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),\n            \"std\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),\n            \"n_tokens_kept\": max(1, int(N_VISION_TOKENS * k)),\n        }\n\n    print(f\"\\n[PRUNING RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"{'k':>6} {'tokens':>6} {'norm_loss':>12} {'std':>10}\")\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        r = pruning_results[str(k)]\n        print(f\"  {k:.2f}   {r['n_tokens_kept']:>4}   {r['normalized_loss']:>10.6f}   {r['std']:>8.6f}\")\n\n    handle.remove()\n\n    return entropy_results, pruning_results\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"OpenVLA-OFT Token Importance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    images, task_names, unique_tasks = load_libero_images()\n    if not images:\n        print(\"ERROR: No images loaded!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    model, processor = load_model()\n    entropy_results, pruning_results = run_analysis(model, processor, images, task_names, unique_tasks)\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    entropy_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"openvla_oft_attention_entropy_perlayer.json\")\n    with open(entropy_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(entropy_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\n[*]
